📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期禽流感病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • HPAI 相关的 死亡率 从年轻饲养者中的约 5-10% 增加到最年长个体中的 40% 以上。 [10.1038/s41467-026-76726-7]。

  • HPAI-related mortality increased from approximately 5–10% in young breeders to more than 40% in the oldest individuals. [10.1038/s41467-026-76726-7].
  • Compared to the WHO-recommended candidate vaccine virus (CVV), AL/39, cHANA elicited more potent cross-reactive antibody responses and T cell immunity in mice.
  • The study identified four highly conserved, functionally constrained residues (G70, G132, N167, and M173) that remained nearly invariant over decades.
  • NIID-002 showed markedly reduced pathogenicity compared to its parental strain, with no lethal infection in mice and only minimal weight loss and limited viral replication in ferrets.

Journal: Nature communications  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-19  |  DOI: 10.1038/s41467-026-76726-7  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 17, 1

禽流感 在长寿海鸟中放大了与年龄相关的 死亡率。

Avian influenza amplified age-related mortality in a long-lived seabird.
作者： Wouter Courtens, Mónika Ballmann, Ruben Fijn, Morten Frederiksen, Susanne Kühn, Luc Lens, Mardik Leopold, Sander Lilipaly, Florian Packmor, Eric W. M. Stienen
摘要中文翻译摘要 近年来,高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) A (H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒在全球海鸟中引起了大规模 死亡率 事件,引起了人们对低繁殖率的长寿物种的关注。利用 28 年的鸣响数据以及 2022 年欧洲西北部三明治燕鸥(Thalasseus sandvicensis)大规模 死亡率 事件的个体水平数据,我们发现 死亡率 偏向于老年个体,而没有观察到性别偏见。HPAI 相关的 死亡率 从年轻饲养者中的约 5-10% 增加到最年长个体中的 40% 以上。这种年龄偏向的损失可能会从人群中剔除最有经验的人。我们的研究结果强调了一种以前未被充分认识的机制,HPAI 暴发 可能通过该机制损害长寿鸟类种群的恢复能力。

Avian influenza amplified age-related mortality in a long-lived seabird.

Authors: Wouter Courtens, Mónika Ballmann, Ruben Fijn, Morten Frederiksen, Susanne Kühn, Luc Lens, Mardik Leopold, Sander Lilipaly, Florian Packmor, Eric W. M. Stienen
Original abstract (English)
Abstract In recent years, highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A (H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have caused mass mortality events in seabirds worldwide, raising concern for long-lived species with low reproductive rates. Using 28 years of ringing data together with individual-level data from the 2022 mass mortality event in northwestern European Sandwich terns ( Thalasseus sandvicensis ), we show that mortality was biased towards older individuals, while no sex bias was observed. HPAI-related mortality increased from approximately 5–10% in young breeders to more than 40% in the oldest individuals. This age-biased loss likely removed the most experienced individuals from the population. Our findings highlight a previously underappreciated mechanism through which HPAI outbreaks may impair the resilience of long-lived avian populations.
View research seven-element analysis

Abstract In recent years, highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A (H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have caused mass mortality events in seabirds worldwide, raising concern for long-lived species with low reproductive rates.
Design and population

HPAI-related mortality increased from approximately 5–10% in young breeders to more than 40% in the oldest individuals.

Limitations and evidence strength

源自日本首例哺乳动物进化枝 2.3.4.4b A/H5N1 高致病性禽流感 病毒的新型候选 疫苗 病毒

A novel candidate vaccine virus derived from Japan's first mammalian case of clade 2.3.4.4b A/H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza virus
作者： Yasushi Suzuki, Tomoko Arita, Masayuki Shirakura, Noriko Kishida, Kaori Sano, Shinji Watanabe, Noriyo Nagata, Yusuke Sakai, Naoko Iwata‐Yoshikawa, Tadaki Suzuki
摘要中文翻译用于大流行前准备的候选 疫苗 病毒 (CVV) 的开发需要减弱致病性,同时保持免疫原性。在本研究中,我们开发并表征了 NIID-002,这是一种源自 A/Ezo 红狐/北海道/1/2022(H5N1;进化枝 2.3.4.4b)的重配病毒,以评估其作为候选 疫苗 的适用性。与其亲本菌株相比,NIID-002 的致病性显着降低,同时保留了与其他进化枝 2.3.4.4b CVV 相当的广泛抗原反应性和蛋白质产量。在哺乳动物模型中,NIID-002表现出很强的减毒作用,对小鼠没有造成致命的感染,在雪貂中仅造成最小​​的体重减轻,且病毒复制有限。针对 NIID-002 的抗血清与最近的野生型 H5N1 分离株广泛反应,表明潜在的广泛保护作用。蛋白质产量分析证实其生产效率与同一进化枝内的其他 CVV 相当,支持其大规模 疫苗 生产的可行性。总体而言,NIID-002满足了CVV大流行防范的关键要求,结合了降低的致病性、广泛的抗原反应性和足够的生产效率。这些发现凸显了其作为候选 H5N1 疫苗 的潜力,并强调了对 监测 的持续需求以及流感 疫苗 策略的完善,以应对不断变化的病毒威胁。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种候选 疫苗 病毒(CVV),通过减弱致病性同时保持免疫原性,为大流行前做好准备,满足对 疫苗 有效对抗 禽流感 A 病毒的需求。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 NIID-002 的开发和表征,NIID-002 是一种源自 A/Ezo 红狐/北海道/1/2022(H5N1;进化枝 2.3.4.4b)的重配病毒,以评估其作为哺乳动物模型中候选 疫苗 的适用性。
核心方法
使用小鼠和雪貂等哺乳动物模型评估病毒复制和感染结果,将 NIID-002 与其亲本毒株在致病性、抗原反应性和蛋白质产量方面进行比较。
主要结果
与亲本毒株相比,NIID-002 的致病性显着降低,在小鼠中没有致命的感染,在雪貂中仅出现最小的体重减轻和有限的病毒复制。与其他进化枝 2.3.4.4b CVV 相比,它保留了广泛的抗原反应性和蛋白质产量。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,NIID-002 是一种有前途的候选 疫苗 病毒,因为它平衡了致病性降低与免疫原性和生产效率,这对于大流行的防范至关重要。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果支持使用 NIID-002 作为 H5N1 禽流感 的候选 疫苗,有助于制定针对不断演变的病毒威胁的 监测 和 疫苗 生产的有效策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于哺乳动物模型,还需要进一步的研究来证实其对人类的功效。证据是中等的,因为这些发现是基于体内实验和抗原反应数据。

A novel candidate vaccine virus derived from Japan's first mammalian case of clade 2.3.4.4b A/H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza virus

Authors: Yasushi Suzuki, Tomoko Arita, Masayuki Shirakura, Noriko Kishida, Kaori Sano, Shinji Watanabe, Noriyo Nagata, Yusuke Sakai, Naoko Iwata‐Yoshikawa, Tadaki Suzuki
Original abstract (English)
The development of candidate vaccine viruses (CVVs) for pre-pandemic preparedness requires attenuation of pathogenicity while maintaining immunogenicity. In this study, we developed and characterized NIID-002, a reassortant virus derived from A/Ezo red fox/Hokkaido/1/2022 (H5N1; clade 2.3.4.4b), to evaluate its suitability as a candidate vaccine. NIID-002 exhibited markedly reduced pathogenicity compared with its parental strain, while retaining broad antigenic reactivity and protein yield comparable to other clade 2.3.4.4b CVVs. In mammalian models, NIID-002 demonstrated strong attenuation, causing no lethal infection in mice and only minimal weight loss with limited viral replication in ferrets. Antisera raised against NIID-002 reacted broadly with recent wild-type H5N1 isolates, suggesting potential broad protection. Protein yield analysis confirmed a production efficiency comparable to that of other CVVs within the same clade, supporting its feasibility for large-scale vaccine manufacturing. Overall, NIID-002 fulfills the key requirements for the pandemic preparedness of CVV, combining reduced pathogenicity, broad antigenic reactivity, and adequate production efficiency. These findings highlight its potential as a candidate H5N1 vaccine and underscore the continued need for surveillance and refinement of influenza vaccine strategies to address evolving viral threats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a candidate vaccine virus (CVV) for pre-pandemic preparedness by attenuating pathogenicity while maintaining immunogenicity, addressing the need for effective vaccines against avian influenza A viruses.
Design and population
The study involved the development and characterization of NIID-002, a reassortant virus derived from A/Ezo red fox/Hokkaido/1/2022 (H5N1; clade 2.3.4.4b), to evaluate its suitability as a candidate vaccine in mammalian models.
Core methods
NIID-002 was compared with its parental strain in terms of pathogenicity, antigenic reactivity, and protein yield, using mammalian models such as mice and ferrets to assess viral replication and infection outcomes.
Main results
NIID-002 showed markedly reduced pathogenicity compared to its parental strain, with no lethal infection in mice and only minimal weight loss and limited viral replication in ferrets. It retained broad antigenic reactivity and protein yield comparable to other clade 2.3.4.4b CVVs.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that NIID-002 is a promising candidate vaccine virus, as it balances reduced pathogenicity with immunogenicity and production efficiency, which are critical for pandemic preparedness.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results support the use of NIID-002 as a candidate vaccine for H5N1 avian influenza, contributing to the development of effective strategies for surveillance and vaccine production against evolving viral threats.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to mammalian models, and further research is needed to confirm its efficacy in humans. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on in vivo experiments and antigenic reactivity data.

掺入表位优化表面蛋白的二价灭活流感 疫苗 赋予针对 H9N2 流感病毒的交叉保护性免疫力。

A bivalent inactivated influenza vaccine incorporating epitope-optimized surface proteins confers cross-protective immunity against H9N2 influenza virus.
作者： Mengchan Hao, Yiwei Guan, Meng Xu, Wenxue Yang, Yanhai Wang, Yuan Zhang, Jianjun Chen
摘要中文翻译H9N2 禽流感 病毒(AIV)给全球家禽业造成了重大经济损失,并对人类构成人畜共患威胁。疫苗接种 构成了预防和控制 H9N2 AIV 的关键策略。然而,病毒持续存在的抗原性 进化 对现有 疫苗 的保护功效提出了持续的挑战。因此,开发能够引发交叉反应的 免疫应答 的广泛保护性 H9N2 流感 疫苗 对于减轻疾病负担和大流行风险至关重要。在这里,我们通过组合两种单独拯救的嵌合灭活病毒 cHANA1 和 cHANA2,开发了一种二价嵌合灭活 疫苗,命名为 cHANA。每种重组病毒都携带一组 Epigraph 设计的 HA 和 NA 免疫原,这两组免疫原根据全球 H9N2 HA 和 NA 序列数据集进行了计算优化,以在整个 H9N2 病毒群体的表位覆盖方面相互补充。与 WHO 推荐的候选 疫苗 病毒 (CVV)、AL/39 相比,cHANA 在小鼠体内引发了更有效的交叉反应抗体反应和 T 细胞免疫。此外,它还能针对异源 H9N2 病毒的致命攻击产生有效的交叉保护,并显着降低小鼠的肺部病毒载量。通过赋予广泛的保护性免疫力,该 疫苗 代表了一种有前途的通用 疫苗 候选药物,用于控制 H9N2 暴发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
H9N2 禽流感 病毒(AIV)给全球家禽业造成了重大经济损失,并对人类构成人畜共患威胁。该研究旨在解决开发 疫苗 的挑战,该 疫苗 可以提供针对 H9N2 AIV 的交叉保护性免疫力。
设计与对象
该研究涉及通过组合两种单独拯救的嵌合灭活病毒 cHANA1 和 cHANA2 来开发二价嵌合灭活 疫苗(cHANA)。疫苗 在小鼠身上进行了测试,以评估其对 H9N2 流感病毒的保护功效。
核心方法
疫苗 是通过计算优化全球 H9N2 序列数据集中的 HA 和 NA 免疫原而开发的。将两组免疫原组合起来创建嵌合体 疫苗,并通过抗体和 T 细胞反应测量来评估其在小鼠中的免疫原性。
主要结果
与 WHO 推荐的候选 疫苗 病毒 (CVV)、AL/39 相比,cHANA 在小鼠体内引发了更有效的交叉反应抗体反应和 T 细胞免疫。它还提供了针对异源 H9N2 病毒致命攻击的有效交叉保护,并显着降低了肺部病毒载量。
解释与创新
结果表明,chHANA 疫苗 可以引发针对 H9N2 病毒的更广泛的 免疫应答,为 疫苗 开发提供了一种新方法,可以增强针对抗原多样化毒株的交叉保护。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,chHANA 可能是控制 H9N2 暴发 的有前景的通用 疫苗 候选药物,对改进 监测 以及家禽和人类的预防策略具有潜在影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠体内测试,结果可能无法完全转化为人类应用。基于与 WHO 推荐的 疫苗 的直接比较以及观察到的交叉保护作用,证据是强有力的。

A bivalent inactivated influenza vaccine incorporating epitope-optimized surface proteins confers cross-protective immunity against H9N2 influenza virus.

Authors: Mengchan Hao, Yiwei Guan, Meng Xu, Wenxue Yang, Yanhai Wang, Yuan Zhang, Jianjun Chen
Original abstract (English)
The H9N2 avian influenza virus (AIV) has caused substantial economic losses to the global poultry industry and poses a zoonotic threat to humans. Vaccination constitutes a pivotal strategy for the prevention and control of H9N2 AIVs. However, the ongoing antigenic evolution of the viruses pose a persistent challenge to the protective efficacy of existing vaccines. Therefore, the development of a broadly protective H9N2 influenza vaccine capable of eliciting cross-reactive immune responses is crucial for mitigating both the disease burden and the risk of pandemics. Here, we developed a bivalent chimeric inactivated vaccine, designated cHANA, by combining two individually rescued chimeric inactivated viruses, cHANA1 and cHANA2. Each recombinant virus carries one set of Epigraph-designed HA and NA immunogens, and the two sets were computationally optimized from global H9N2 HA and NA sequence datasets to complement each other in epitope coverage across the H9N2 viral population. Compared to the WHO-recommended candidate vaccine virus (CVV), AL/39, cHANA elicited more potent cross-reactive antibody responses and T cell immunity in mice. Furthermore, it elicited effective cross-protection against lethal challenge with heterologous H9N2 virus and significantly reduced pulmonary viral loads of mice. By conferring broad protective immunity, this vaccine represents a promising universal vaccine candidate for controlling H9N2 outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The H9N2 avian influenza virus (AIV) has caused substantial economic losses to the global poultry industry and poses a zoonotic threat to humans. The study aimed to address the challenge of developing a vaccine that can provide cross-protective immunity against H9N2 AIVs.
Design and population
The study involved the development of a bivalent chimeric inactivated vaccine, cHANA, by combining two individually rescued chimeric inactivated viruses, cHANA1 and cHANA2. The vaccine was tested in mice to evaluate its protective efficacy against H9N2 influenza virus.
Core methods
The vaccine was developed by computationally optimizing HA and NA immunogens from global H9N2 sequence datasets. The two sets of immunogens were combined to create a chimeric vaccine, and its immunogenicity was assessed in mice through antibody and T cell response measurements.
Main results
Compared to the WHO-recommended candidate vaccine virus (CVV), AL/39, cHANA elicited more potent cross-reactive antibody responses and T cell immunity in mice. It also provided effective cross-protection against lethal challenge with heterologous H9N2 virus and significantly reduced pulmonary viral loads.
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrate that the cHANA vaccine can elicit broader immune responses against H9N2 viruses, offering a novel approach to vaccine development that may enhance cross-protection against antigenically diverse strains.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that cHANA could be a promising universal vaccine candidate for controlling H9N2 outbreaks, with potential implications for improving surveillance and prevention strategies in both poultry and human populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vivo testing in mice, and the results may not fully translate to human applications. The evidence is strong based on the direct comparison with the WHO-recommended vaccine and the observed cross-protective effects.

一种针对保守血凝素表位的双特异性抗体可提供针对 H7 禽流感 的广泛保护,并通过局部呼吸递送加速病毒清除。

A bispecific antibody targeting conserved hemagglutinin epitopes confers broad protection against H7 avian influenza and accelerates viral clearance via localized respiratory delivery.
作者： Haibo Wu, Fan Yang, Ping Wang, Jiamin Fu, Jun Zhang, Jing Guo, Linfang Cheng, Fumin Liu, Nanping Wu, Hangping Yao
摘要中文翻译人畜共患 H7 禽流感 病毒的持续抗原漂移仍然是全球公共卫生的重大威胁。虽然针对血凝素 (HA) 头部的单克隆抗体 (mAb) 可提供有效的中和作用,但其功效往往会因病毒快速逃逸而受到削弱。在这项研究中,我们使用一组 30 种鼠 mAb 系统地绘制了 H7 HA 头结构域的抗原结构图。通过将表位作图与超过 2,500 个分离株的纵向进化分析相结合,我们鉴定了高度保守、功能受限的残基,特别是 G70、G132、N167 和 M173,这些残基几十年来几乎保持不变。利用这些见解,我们设计了一种嵌合双特异性抗体 (BsAb-H7),以同时接合两个不同的、不重叠的保守表位。BsAb-H7 在体外对不同的 H7N9、H7N7 和 H7N3 亚型表现出广泛的中和广度,并表现出优异的体内药代动力学特征。在致死性小鼠攻击模型中,BsAb-H7 提供了针对异源 H7N7 感染的强大预防和治疗保护。值得注意的是,局部鼻内给药通过在延迟干预窗口期间加速病毒清除和解决肺部免疫病理学问题,显着优于全身给药。这些结果表明,保守HA表位的双特异性靶向有效增强抗病毒药物广度并限制免疫逃逸,为开发针对快速进化的H7流感病毒的广泛保护性疗法提供了有前景的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人畜共患 H7 禽流感 病毒的持续抗原漂移仍然是全球公共卫生的重大威胁。这项研究旨在解决开发针对快速进化的 H7 流感病毒的广泛保护性疗法的挑战。
设计与对象
这项研究涉及使用一组 30 种鼠类单克隆抗体对 H7 HA 头结构域的抗原结构进行系统绘图。该研究是在实验室环境中使用体外和体内模型(包括致死性小鼠攻击模型)进行的,以评估工程双特异性抗体的功效。
核心方法
该研究使用了 2,500 多个分离株的表位作图和纵向进化分析来鉴定保守残基。嵌合双特异性抗体 (BsAb-H7) 经过设计,可靶向 H7 HA 头域上两个不同的、不重叠的保守表位。在体外和体内评估了抗体的中和广度和药代动力学特征。
主要结果
该研究鉴定了四个高度保守、功能受限的残基(G70、G132、N167 和 M173),这些残基几十年来几乎保持不变。BsAb-H7 在体外表现出对 H7N9、H7N7 和 H7N3 亚型的广泛中和广度,并表现出优异的体内药代动力学特征。
解释与创新
作者解释说,保守 HA 表位的双特异性靶向有效增强了 抗病毒药物 的广度并限制了免疫逃逸。这种方法代表了一种针对快速进化的 H7 流感病毒开发广泛保护性疗法的新策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对保守 HA 表位的双特异性抗体可能是开发 H7 流感病毒疗法的有前途的策略。这可以为未来关于人畜共患 H7 感染的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据基于体外和体内模型,可能无法完全代表人类 免疫应答。结果得到详细表位作图和药代动力学分析的支持,为研究结果提供了适度的可信度。

A bispecific antibody targeting conserved hemagglutinin epitopes confers broad protection against H7 avian influenza and accelerates viral clearance via localized respiratory delivery.

Authors: Haibo Wu, Fan Yang, Ping Wang, Jiamin Fu, Jun Zhang, Jing Guo, Linfang Cheng, Fumin Liu, Nanping Wu, Hangping Yao
Original abstract (English)
The continuous antigenic drift of zoonotic H7 avian influenza viruses remains a significant global public health threat. While monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) targeting the hemagglutinin (HA) head offer potent neutralization, their efficacy is often undermined by rapid viral escape. In this study, we systematically mapped the antigenic architecture of the H7 HA head domain using a panel of 30 murine mAbs. By integrating epitope mapping with longitudinal evolutionary analysis of over 2,500 isolates, we identified highly conserved, functionally constrained residues, specifically G70, G132, N167, and M173, that remained nearly invariant over decades. Leveraging these insights, we engineered a chimeric bispecific antibody (BsAb-H7) to simultaneously engage two distinct, non-overlapping conserved epitopes. BsAb-H7 demonstrated broad neutralization breadth across divergent H7N9, H7N7, and H7N3 subtypes in vitro and exhibited a superior in vivo pharmacokinetic profile. In lethal murine challenge models, BsAb-H7 provided robust prophylactic and therapeutic protection against heterologous H7N7 infection. Notably, localized intranasal administration significantly outperformed systemic delivery by accelerating viral clearance and resolving pulmonary immunopathology during delayed intervention windows. These results demonstrate that bispecific targeting of conserved HA epitopes effectively enhances antiviral breadth and limits immune escape, providing a promising strategy for the development of broadly protective therapeutics against rapidly evolving H7 influenza viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The continuous antigenic drift of zoonotic H7 avian influenza viruses remains a significant global public health threat. This study aimed to address the challenge of developing broadly protective therapeutics against rapidly evolving H7 influenza viruses.
Design and population
This study involved systematic mapping of the antigenic architecture of the H7 HA head domain using a panel of 30 murine mAbs. The research was conducted in a laboratory setting with in vitro and in vivo models, including lethal murine challenge models, to evaluate the efficacy of the engineered bispecific antibody.
Core methods
The study used epitope mapping and longitudinal evolutionary analysis of over 2,500 isolates to identify conserved residues. A chimeric bispecific antibody (BsAb-H7) was engineered to target two distinct, non-overlapping conserved epitopes on the H7 HA head domain. The antibody's neutralization breadth and pharmacokinetic profile were assessed in vitro and in vivo.
Main results
The study identified four highly conserved, functionally constrained residues (G70, G132, N167, and M173) that remained nearly invariant over decades. BsAb-H7 demonstrated broad neutralization breadth across H7N9, H7N7, and H7N3 subtypes in vitro and showed a superior in vivo pharmacokinetic profile.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that bispecific targeting of conserved HA epitopes effectively enhances antiviral breadth and limits immune escape. This approach represents a novel strategy for developing broadly protective therapeutics against rapidly evolving H7 influenza viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that bispecific antibodies targeting conserved HA epitopes could be a promising strategy for the development of therapeutics against H7 influenza viruses. This could inform future research on surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of zoonotic H7 infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on in vitro and in vivo models, which may not fully represent human immune responses. The results are supported by detailed epitope mapping and pharmacokinetic analysis, providing moderate confidence in the findings.

与 D1.1 相比,人源 禽流感 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 基因型 B3.13 在雪貂中的致病性和接触传播性增强

Enhanced Pathogenicity and Contact Transmissibility of Human-origin Avian Influenza H5N1 Clade 2.3.4.4b Genotype B3.13 Compared to D1.1 in Ferrets
作者： Elsayed M. Abdelwhab, Ramya S. Barre, Mahmoud Bayoumi, Alvaro Padrón, Hossein Batebi, Vinay Shivanna, Roy N. Platt, Fiona Burmeister, Joshua Castro, Arash Rahmani
摘要中文翻译摘要 自 2020 年出现以来,H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 的多种基因型已被鉴定,其中 B3.13 和 D1.1 在美国出现,成为两个主要且令人担忧的基因型。然而,它们在哺乳动物中的相对致病性和传播性尚未完全阐明。我们在雪貂感染模型和 传播 中比较了德克萨斯州 B3.13 (A/Texas/37/2024;HPhTX B3.13) 和路易斯安那州 D1.1 (A/Louisiana/12/2024;HPhLA D1.1) 引起的前两例人类 H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 病例的致病性和传播性。HPhTX B3.13 感染导致更严重的临床疾病和增强的病毒脱落,有证据表明 传播 相对于 HPhLA D1.1 有所增加。组织病理学分析显示,感染 HPhTX B3.13 的动物出现更广泛的肺部病理学变化,与病毒载量和炎症反应增加一致。重要的是,两种基因型在针对候选 疫苗 病毒 (CVV) 株的雪貂血清的反应性、受体结合特性或神经氨酸酶 (NA) 活性和热稳定性方面没有显着差异。全 基因组 测序显示感染后 HPhTX B3.13 或 传播 中没有适应性 突变。相比之下,HPhLA D1.1 在受感染的雪貂中快速获得了哺乳动物适应性的 突变 E627K,并在唯一致命的接触动物中快速获得了 E627K 和 Q194K。突变 都与增强的聚合酶活性相关,计算分析表明它们增强了与哺乳动物 宿主 因子 ANP32A 和 B 的相互作用。我们的研究结果表明,B3.13 已经很好地适应了哺乳动物感染,传播 而 D1.1 通过快速获得适应性 突变 保留了进化潜力,突出了与人畜共患病风险评估和大流行防范相关的重要基因型特异性差异。意义 H5N1 流感病毒在基因上继续多样化,同时扩展到哺乳动物 宿主,增加了病毒适应和人畜共患 传播(包括人类)的机会。然而,主要分支 2.3.4.4b 基因型在哺乳动物中感染、传播和进化的能力是否有所不同仍然知之甚少。使用流感感染的雪貂模型和 传播,我们证明当前流行的 B3.13 和 D1.1 基因型表现出不同的致病性和 传播 特征,尽管保留了相似的受体结合特征、NA 功能和抗原谱。虽然 B3.13 很容易在雪貂中感染和传播,并且不会产生与复制增加和 传播 相关的进一步适应性 突变,但 D1.1 在单次感染和/或 传播 事件后迅速获得哺乳动物适应性 突变,突出了其进化潜力。这些发现表明,基因型特异性生物学特性可以独立于抗原相似性影响人畜共患风险,并强调将表型特征与 基因组监测 相结合以改善大流行防范和指导公共卫生风险评估的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自 2020 年出现以来,已鉴定出 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的多种基因型,其中 B3.13 和 D1.1 在美国出现,成为两个主要且令人担忧的基因型。然而,它们在哺乳动物中的相对致病性和传播性尚未完全阐明。
设计与对象
我们在雪貂感染模型和 传播 中比较了德克萨斯州 B3.13 (A/Texas/37/2024;HPhTX B3.13) 和路易斯安那州 D1.1 (A/Louisiana/12/2024;HPhLA D1.1) 引起的前两例人类 H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 病例的致病性和传播性。
核心方法
全 基因组 测序显示感染后 HPhTX B3.13 或 传播 中没有适应性 突变。使用流感感染的雪貂模型和 传播,我们证明当前流行的 B3.13 和 D1.1 基因型表现出不同的致病性和 传播 特征,尽管保留了相似的受体结合特征、NA 功能和抗原谱。
主要结果
HPhTX B3.13 感染导致更严重的临床疾病和增强的病毒脱落,有证据表明 传播 相对于 HPhLA D1.1 有所增加。组织病理学分析显示,感染 HPhTX B3.13 的动物出现更广泛的肺部病理学变化,与病毒载量和炎症反应增加一致。相比之下,HPhLA D1.1 在受感染的雪貂中快速获得了哺乳动物适应性的 突变 E627K,并在唯一致命的接触动物中快速获得了 E627K 和 Q194K。
解释与创新
我们的研究结果表明,B3.13 已经很好地适应了哺乳动物感染和 传播,而 D1.1 通过快速获得适应性 突变 保留了进化潜力,突出了与人畜共患病风险评估和大流行防范相关的重要基因型特异性差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,基因型特异性生物学特性可以独立于抗原相似性影响人畜共患风险,并强调将表型特征与 基因组监测 相结合以改善大流行防范和指导公共卫生风险评估的重要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或证据强度的局限性。

Enhanced Pathogenicity and Contact Transmissibility of Human-origin Avian Influenza H5N1 Clade 2.3.4.4b Genotype B3.13 Compared to D1.1 in Ferrets

Authors: Elsayed M. Abdelwhab, Ramya S. Barre, Mahmoud Bayoumi, Alvaro Padrón, Hossein Batebi, Vinay Shivanna, Roy N. Platt, Fiona Burmeister, Joshua Castro, Arash Rahmani
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Since its emergence in 2020, multiple genotypes of the H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b have been identified, with B3.13 and D1.1 emerging in the USA as two major and concerning genotypes. However, their relative pathogenicity and transmissibility in mammals have not been fully elucidated. We compared the pathogenicity and transmissibility of the first two human H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b cases caused by B3.13 in Texas (A/Texas/37/2024; HPhTX B3.13) and D1.1 in Louisiana (A/Louisiana/12/2024; HPhLA D1.1) in a ferret model of infection and transmission. HPhTX B3.13 infection resulted in more severe clinical disease and enhanced viral shedding, with evidence of increased transmission relative to HPhLA D1.1. Histopathological analysis revealed more extensive lung pathology in animals infected with HPhTX B3.13, consistent with increased viral loads and inflammatory responses. Importantly, both genotypes showed no significant differences in reactivity to ferret sera raised against candidate vaccine virus (CVV) strains, receptor binding properties, or neuraminidase (NA) activity and thermostability. Whole-genome sequencing revealed no adaptive mutations in HPhTX B3.13 following infection or transmission. In contrast, HPhLA D1.1 showed rapid acquisition of the mammalian-adaptive mutation E627K in infected ferrets and both E627K and Q194K in the only fatal contact animal. Both mutations were associated with enhanced polymerase activity and computational analyses suggested that they enhance interactions with the mammalian host factors ANP32A and B. Our findings indicate that B3.13 is already well adapted for mammalian infection and transmission whereas D1.1 retains evolutionary potential through the rapid acquisition of adaptive mutations, highlighting important genotype-specific differences relevant to zoonotic risk assessment and pandemic preparedness. Significance Influenza H5N1 viruses continue to diversify genetically while expanding into mammalian hosts, increasing opportunities for viral adaptation and zoonotic transmission, including humans. However, whether the predominant clade 2.3.4.4b genotype differs in its capacity to infect, transmit, and evolve in mammals remains poorly understood. Using the ferret model of influenza infection and transmission, we demonstrated that the currently circulating B3.13 and D1.1 genotypes exhibit distinct pathogenic and transmission characteristics despite retaining similar receptor-binding characteristics, NA functions, and antigenic profiles. While B3.13 readily infects and transmits in ferrets and does not develop further adaptive mutations associated with increased replication and transmission, D1.1 rapidly acquires mammalian-adaptive mutations after a single infection and/or transmission event, highlighting its evolutionary potential. These findings show that genotype-specific biological properties can influence zoonotic risk independently of antigenic similarity and emphasize the importance of integrating phenotypic characterization with genomic surveillance to improve pandemic preparedness and guide public health risk assessment.

Question and background
Since its emergence in 2020, multiple genotypes of the H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b have been identified, with B3.13 and D1.1 emerging in the USA as two major and concerning genotypes. However, their relative pathogenicity and transmissibility in mammals have not been fully elucidated.

The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study design or evidence strength.

超越进入:评估 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 在澳大利亚的内部传播风险

Beyond Entry: Evaluating the Internal Spread Risk of highly pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI) H5N1 in Australia
作者： Catarina do Carmo Norte dos Santos, David Heslop, Patricia Lee, James G Wood, Md Saiful Islam
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 病毒 H5N1 目前正在全球范围内传播,大洋洲仍然是唯一基本上未受影响的大陆,直到最近在西澳大利亚和南澳大利亚发现了候鸟阳性病例,最近在新南威尔士州发现了疑似病例。此外,赫德岛的 H5N1 病毒已在象海豹幼崽中造成广泛的 死亡率。迁徙水禽和滨鸟被认为是 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 的关键 媒介,将澳大利亚与全球 传播 网络连接起来。在这里,我们开发了一个基于代理的模型来预测 H5N1 在澳大利亚不同地区潜在入侵后的传播。我们的结果表明,入侵区域强烈影响 暴发 的传播和持续时间。H5N1 在澳大利亚的传播给家禽 暴发 和潜在的 跨物种溢出 进入野生动物种群带来了巨大风险,特别是在生态敏感的繁殖区。这些发现为告知国家准备情况、指导有针对性的 监测 以及加强 One Health 战略以减轻 HPAI 对澳大利亚的影响提供了重要见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 在澳大利亚的内部传播风险,因为该病毒最近在候鸟中被发现,并对家禽和野生动物种群构成威胁。
设计与对象
我们开发了一个基于代理的模型来预测 H5N1 可能入侵澳大利亚不同地区后的传播,重点关注候鸟及其在将国家与全球 传播 网络连接起来方面的作用。
核心方法
该研究使用基于代理的模型来模拟 H5N1 的传播,结合了澳大利亚各地区的迁徙模式和潜在入侵点的数据。
主要结果
结果表明,入侵区域显着影响 H5N1 暴发 在澳大利亚境内的传播和持续时间。
解释与创新
这些发现强调了入侵位置在确定 H5N1 传播动态方面的重要性,为理解和预测其内部 传播 提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
该研究为国家准备、有针对性的 监测 和 One Health 战略提供了重要见解,以减轻 HPAI 对澳大利亚的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于模拟模型,该模型可能无法完全捕捉现实世界的复杂性,并且证据基于报告的案例和一般假设,从而得出适度的置信度评估。

Beyond Entry: Evaluating the Internal Spread Risk of highly pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI) H5N1 in Australia

Authors: Catarina do Carmo Norte dos Santos, David Heslop, Patricia Lee, James G Wood, Md Saiful Islam
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) virus H5N1 is currently spreading worldwide, with Oceania remaining the only continent largely unaffected until recently, when positive cases in migratory birds were detected in Western Australia and South Australia, and, more recently, a suspected case in New South Wales. Furthermore, H5N1 in Heard Island has caused widespread mortality in elephant seal pups. Migratory waterfowl and shorebirds, recognised as key vectors of avian influenza viruses (AIV), connect Australia with global transmission networks. Here, we developed an agent-based model to predict the spread of H5N1 following potential incursions across different regions of Australia. Our results suggest that the incursion region strongly affects outbreak spread and duration. The spread of H5N1 in Australia creates a substantial risk for poultry outbreaks and potential spillover into wildlife populations, particularly in ecologically sensitive breeding areas. These findings provide critical insights for informing national preparedness, guiding targeted surveillance, and strengthening One Health strategies to mitigate the impact of HPAI in Australia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the internal spread risk of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 in Australia, as the virus has recently been detected in migratory birds and poses a threat to poultry and wildlife populations.
Design and population
An agent-based model was developed to predict the spread of H5N1 following potential incursions across different regions of Australia, focusing on migratory birds and their role in connecting the country with global transmission networks.
Core methods
The study used an agent-based model to simulate the spread of H5N1, incorporating data on migratory patterns and potential incursion points across various regions of Australia.
Main results
The results indicate that the region of incursion significantly influences the spread and duration of H5N1 outbreaks within Australia.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the importance of incursion location in determining the spread dynamics of H5N1, offering a novel approach to understanding and predicting its internal transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides critical insights for national preparedness, targeted surveillance, and One Health strategies to mitigate the impact of HPAI in Australia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on a simulated model, which may not fully capture real-world complexities, and the evidence is based on reported cases and general assumptions, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

高致病性禽流感 病毒多次传入北极地区:斯瓦尔巴群岛和扬马延群岛,2022-2025 年

Multiple introductions of highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses into the High Arctic: Svalbard and Jan Mayen, 2022–2025
作者： Silje Granstad, Ragnhild Tønnessen, Bjørnar Ytrehus, Sébastien Descamps, Børge Moe, Hallvard Strøm, Geir Wing Gabrielsen, Sveinn Are Hanssen, Egil Rønning, Ole-Herman Tronerud
摘要中文翻译摘要 2022 年至 2025 年间,在斯瓦尔巴群岛和扬马延岛的 野生鸟类 和哺乳动物中检测到了进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV),包括四种不同的 H5 欧亚 (EA) 基因型。我们描述了它们的 流行病学 和基因组特征,以增进对高北极地区 HPAIV 发生和 传播 的了解。2022 年的最初病例发生在夏季,涉及斯瓦尔巴群岛和扬马延岛的灰鸥 (Larus hyperboreus) 和大贼鸥 (Stercorarius skua),这是高北极地区首次发现高致病性禽流感病毒。鉴定出三种 HPAIV 基因型:EA-2020-C (H5N1)、EA-2021-AB (H5N1) 和 EA-2021-I (H5N5)。2023 年,在更广泛的鸟类中检测到了 HPAIV,另一个研究小组 (Odobenus rosmarus rosmarus) 回顾性地在大西洋海象中检测到了 HPAIV。2023 年确定的基因型为 EA-2020-C (H5N1)、EA-2021-I (H5N5) 和 EA-2022-BB (H5N1)。2024 年没有报告病例。2025 年,在斯瓦尔巴群岛的北极狐 (Vulpes lagopus) 中检测到 EA-2021-I (H5N5),此前在 野生鸟类 中未检测到。这些狐狸表现出神经系统症状,其中一只狐狸的尸检显示其胃中存在羽毛。所有来自北极狐的测序病毒都独特地携带 PB2-E627K 和 PB1-H115Q 的组合,这与哺乳动物的适应有关。多种基因型的检测表明 HPAIV 被重复且独立地引入到这些区域。遗传上不同的病毒株在鸟类密度高的地区共同循环进一步表明,北极繁殖地可能促进当地病毒的扩增、重组,以及随后沿着迁徙飞行路线的传播,包括跨大陆传播。数据摘要 作者确认所有支持数据、代码和协议均已在文章中或通过补充数据文件提供。通过本研究生成的流感 A 整个 基因组 序列可在表 1 中的 GISAID 登录号下找到。支持本研究结果的所有 基因组 序列和相关元数据均可通过持久性数字对象标识符 https://doi.org/10.55876/gis8.260211rq 访问。2022年1月1日至2025年8月31日在斯瓦尔巴群岛和扬马延岛采集的高致病性禽流感和哺乳动物样本中检测到高致病性禽流感病毒(HPAIV)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了紧迫的公共卫生和生态问题,即进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 如何进入并在北极地区持续存在,而此前在北极地区很少检测到。其目的是提高对这些偏远生态系统中 HPAIV 发生和 传播 的了解。
设计与对象
作者于2022年1月1日至2025年8月31日对斯瓦尔巴群岛和扬马延岛的野生鸟类和哺乳动物进行了纵向野外和基因组监测研究。样本采集自多种鸟类、大西洋海象和北极狐。
核心方法
流感 A 对现场样本进行全 基因组 测序,并将序列与相关元数据一起存放在 GISAID 中。使用已建立的进化枝定义和系统发育方法分配基因型。
主要结果
HPAIV 于 2022 年(白云鸥和大贼鸥)、2023 年(多种鸟类和海象)被发现,而非 2024 年,2025 年在出现神经症状和胃部羽毛的北极狐中被发现。流行了四种不同的 H5 欧亚基因型:EA-2020-C (H5N1)、EA-2021-AB (H5N1)、EA-2021-I (H5N5) 和 EA-2022-BB (H5N1)。
解释与创新
不同基因型的重复、独立引入和共同循环表明,北极繁殖地可能会沿着迁徙路线放大、重组和传播高致病性禽流感病毒。在狐狸病毒中检测到哺乳动物适应性 突变(PB2-E627K 和 PB1-H115Q)表明潜在的 宿主 适应性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现凸显了高北极作为 HPAIV 入侵的哨兵地区,并强调需要对北极野生动物实施持续的 基因组监测 和生物安全,以便为全球风险评估和准备提供信息。
局限与证据强度
2024 年没有病例限制了时间推断,而且缺乏所有年份的系统抽样可能会低估真实的流通量。尽管如此,现场 监测 与全 基因组 测序的整合为重复引入和局部扩增提供了中等强度的证据。

Multiple introductions of highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses into the High Arctic: Svalbard and Jan Mayen, 2022–2025

Authors: Silje Granstad, Ragnhild Tønnessen, Bjørnar Ytrehus, Sébastien Descamps, Børge Moe, Hallvard Strøm, Geir Wing Gabrielsen, Sveinn Are Hanssen, Egil Rønning, Ole-Herman Tronerud
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Between 2022 and 2025, highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) of clade 2.3.4.4b, including four distinct H5 Eurasian (EA) genotypes, were detected in wild birds and mammals in the Svalbard Archipelago and on the island of Jan Mayen. We describe their epidemiology and genomic characteristics to improve understanding of HPAIV occurrence and transmission in the High Arctic. The initial cases in 2022 occurred during summer and involved a glaucous gull ( Larus hyperboreus ) and great skuas ( Stercorarius skua ) on Svalbard and Jan Mayen, representing the first detections of HPAIVs in the High Arctic. Three HPAIV genotypes were identified: EA-2020-C (H5N1), EA-2021-AB (H5N1), and EA-2021-I (H5N5). In 2023, HPAIVs were detected in a broader range of bird species, and retrospectively in an Atlantic walrus reported by another research group ( Odobenus rosmarus rosmarus ). Genotypes identified in 2023 were EA-2020-C (H5N1), EA-2021-I (H5N5), and EA-2022-BB (H5N1). No cases were reported in 2024. In 2025, EA-2021-I (H5N5) was detected in Arctic foxes ( Vulpes lagopus ) on Svalbard, without preceding detections in wild birds. The foxes exhibited neurological symptoms, and necropsy of one individual revealed the presence of feathers in its stomach. All sequenced viruses from the Arctic foxes uniquely carried the combination of PB2-E627K and PB1-H115Q, which is associated with mammalian adaptation. The detection of multiple genotypes indicates repeated and independent introductions of HPAIVs into these regions. The co-circulation of genetically distinct virus strains in areas of high bird density further suggests that Arctic breeding grounds may facilitate local viral amplification, reassortment, and subsequent dissemination along migratory flyways, including transcontinental spread. Data summary The authors confirm all supporting data, code and protocols have been provided within the article or through supplementary data files. Influenza A whole genome sequences generated through this study are available under the GISAID accession numbers found in Table 1. All genome sequences and associated metadata supporting the findings of this study can be accessed through the persistent digital object identifier https://doi.org/10.55876/gis8.260211rq . Detections of highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) in wild birds and mammals sampled in the Svalbard Archipelago and on Jan Mayen from January 1 st , 2022, to August 31 st , 2025.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the urgent public-health and ecological question of how highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) of clade 2.3.4.4b were entering and persisting in the High Arctic, where prior detections were rare. It aimed to improve understanding of HPAIV occurrence and transmission in these remote ecosystems.
Design and population
The authors conducted a longitudinal field and genomic surveillance study of wild birds and mammals in the Svalbard Archipelago and on Jan Mayen from January 1, 2022, to August 31, 2025. Samples were collected from multiple bird species, Atlantic walrus, and Arctic foxes.
Core methods
Influenza A whole-genome sequencing was performed on field samples, and sequences were deposited in GISAID with associated metadata. Genotypes were assigned using established clade definitions and phylogenetic methods.
Main results
HPAIVs were detected in 2022 (glaucous gull and great skuas), 2023 (multiple bird species and a walrus), not in 2024, and in 2025 in Arctic foxes with neurological signs and stomach feathers. Four distinct H5 Eurasian genotypes circulated: EA-2020-C (H5N1), EA-2021-AB (H5N1), EA-2021-I (H5N5), and EA-2022-BB (H5N1).
Interpretation and novelty
The repeated, independent introductions and co-circulation of distinct genotypes indicate that Arctic breeding grounds may amplify, reassort, and disseminate HPAIVs along migratory flyways. The detection of mammalian-adaptive mutations (PB2-E627K and PB1-H115Q) in fox viruses suggests potential host adaptation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the High Arctic as a sentinel region for HPAIV incursions and underscore the need for sustained genomic surveillance and biosecurity in Arctic wildlife to inform global risk assessments and preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The absence of cases in 2024 limits temporal inferences, and the lack of systematic sampling across all years may underestimate true circulation. Nonetheless, the integration of field surveillance with whole-genome sequencing provides moderate-strength evidence for repeated introductions and local amplification.

AS01、AS03和明矾对免疫应答对豚鼠GMP级H5N1抗原的佐剂作用比较

Comparison of the adjuvant effect of AS01, AS03 and alum on the immune response to GMP-grade H5N1 antigen in guinea pigs
作者： Emmelie Eckhardt, Josephine Friedrich, Wiebke Lange, Julia Sehl-Ewert, Timm Harder, Jens Teifke, Ger van Zandbergen, Max Bastian
摘要中文翻译摘要 佐剂作为 免疫应答 的增强剂和激活剂,对于 疫苗 的功效至关重要。AS01 是一种掺有皂苷衍生物 QS-21 和解毒脂多糖 MPL-A 的脂质体制剂,而 AS03 是一种基于角鲨烯的水包油乳液,含有抗氧化剂生育酚,它们是现代高效佐剂,成为创新型 疫苗 不可或缺的一部分。在当前的研究中,豚鼠(一种成熟的人类流感感染小动物模型)皮下接种了由 AS01、AS03、氢氧化铝配制或未配制的 H5N1 流感抗原。根据抗原制剂评估对流感抗原特异性的T细胞和B细胞的诱导。所有佐剂制剂均观察到 H5N1 特异性 T 细胞反应,但六个月后在 AS01 和 AS03 免疫组中反应最高。接种 AS03 的豚鼠的流感特异性抗体滴度最高。重要的是,AS03和AS01配制的流感抗原还诱导交叉反应抗体抑制相关的H5N3流感病毒株,但不抑制远处的H5N8流感病毒株。同时,观察到接受鸡蛋产生的 H5N1 裂解抗原与 AS03 组合的动物会产生与鸡成纤维细胞结合的异种反应性调理抗体。没有观察到对人成纤维细胞的反应性。总之,我们的结果证实了与传统明矾或无佐剂制剂相比,AS03 和 AS01 具有优异的佐剂性。AS03 疫苗接种 后异种反应抗体的存在是一个意想不到的观察结果。需要进一步的研究来理解这一发现的重要性并确定其机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较 AS01、AS03 和明矾对 免疫应答 与 GMP 级 H5N1 抗原在豚鼠中的佐剂作用,因为佐剂对于 疫苗 功效至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究在豚鼠(一种成熟的人类流感感染小动物模型)中进行,涉及皮下注射 疫苗接种 和 H5N1 抗原,并与 AS01、AS03、氢氧化铝配制或未配制。
核心方法
根据抗原配方评估对流感抗原特异性的 T 细胞和 B 细胞的诱导,并在接种疫苗的动物中测量 免疫应答。
主要结果
所有佐剂制剂均观察到 H5N1 特异性 T 细胞反应,但六个月后,AS01 和 AS03 免疫组的反应最高。在接种 AS03 的豚鼠中,流感特异性抗体滴度也最高。
解释与创新
结果证实了 AS03 和 AS01 与传统明矾或无佐剂制剂相比具有优异的佐剂性,凸显了它们在 疫苗 开发中的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,AS01 和 AS03 可能是提高 疫苗 对 H5N1 的疗效的有价值的佐剂,这对开发更有效的流感 疫苗 具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于豚鼠作为动物模型,证据基于在该模型中观察到的 免疫应答,这可能无法完全转化为人类结果。

Comparison of the adjuvant effect of AS01, AS03 and alum on the immune response to GMP-grade H5N1 antigen in guinea pigs

Authors: Emmelie Eckhardt, Josephine Friedrich, Wiebke Lange, Julia Sehl-Ewert, Timm Harder, Jens Teifke, Ger van Zandbergen, Max Bastian
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Adjuvants function as an enhancer and activator of the immune response and can be of pivotal importance for the efficacy of vaccines. AS01, which is a liposome formulation spiked with the saponin derivate QS-21 and the detoxified lipopolysaccharide MPL-A, and AS03, which is a squalene-based oil in water emulsion that contains antioxidant tocopherol, are modern, highly potent adjuvants that became integral to innovative vaccines. In the current study, guinea pigs –a well-established small animal model for human influenza-infections– were subcutaneously vaccinated with an influenza H5N1-antigen formulated with AS01, AS03, aluminium hydroxide or left unformulated. The induction of T- and B cells specific for the influenza antigen was assessed in dependence of the antigen formulation. H5N1-specific T cell responses were observed with all adjuvant formulations, but after six months were highest in the AS01- and AS03-immunized groups. The influenza specific antibody titres were highest in AS03-vaccinated guinea pigs. Importantly, AS03- and AS01-formulated influenza antigen also induced crossreactive antibodies inhibiting related H5N3- but not a distant H5N8-influenza strain. At the same time, it was observed that animals, which received the egg-produced H5N1 split antigen in combination with AS03, mounted xenoreactive, opsonizing antibodies that bound to chicken fibroblasts. No reactivity was observed to human fibroblasts. In summary, our results confirm the superior adjuvanticity of AS03 and of AS01 as compared to conventional alum or non-adjuvanted formulations. The presence of xenoreactive antibodies after AS03 vaccination was an unexpected observation. Further investigations will be required to appreciate the significance and identify the mechanisms of this findings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare the adjuvant effect of AS01, AS03 and alum on the immune response to GMP-grade H5N1 antigen in guinea pigs, as adjuvants are crucial for vaccine efficacy.
Design and population
The study was conducted in guinea pigs, a well-established small animal model for human influenza infections, and involved subcutaneous vaccination with H5N1 antigen formulated with AS01, AS03, aluminium hydroxide or left unformulated.
Core methods
The induction of T- and B cells specific for the influenza antigen was assessed based on the antigen formulation, with immune responses measured in vaccinated animals.
Main results
H5N1-specific T cell responses were observed with all adjuvant formulations, but after six months, they were highest in the AS01- and AS03-immunized groups. Influenza-specific antibody titres were also highest in AS03-vaccinated guinea pigs.
Interpretation and novelty
The results confirm the superior adjuvanticity of AS03 and AS01 compared to conventional alum or non-adjuvanted formulations, highlighting their potential for vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that AS01 and AS03 could be valuable adjuvants for improving vaccine efficacy against H5N1, which has implications for the development of more effective influenza vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to guinea pigs as an animal model, and the evidence is based on immune responses observed in this model, which may not fully translate to human outcomes.

针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的牛顿差分优化

Newton Divided Difference Optimization for Fingerprint-Based Neural Virtual Screening against Avian Influenza A/H9N2
作者： Siti Amiroch
摘要中文翻译禽流感 A/H9N2 造成持续的人畜共患病和兽医威胁,但用于 抗病毒药物 化合物优先级排序的有效计算工具仍然不发达,特别是在高维神经筛选中的​​优化器行为方面。本研究提出了牛顿除差(NDD)优化,这是一种轻量级正对角曲率感知训练策略,作为针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的新型优化器,目的是评估其在对齐的分子指纹表示和化学结构验证协议中的性能。由 615 种候选活性化合物和 844 种诱饵组成的 1,459 个分子的对齐基准由 EState(79 个特征)、PubChem(881 个特征)和 Klekota–Roth(4,860 个特征)指纹代表,共享相同的分子身份、标签和分割分配。固定多层感知器 (MLP) 分类器使用 NDD 和七个基线优化器在分层、支架键和相似性聚类分割协议下跨五个重复种子进行训练。NDD 在支架密钥和相似性聚类协议下的 Klekota–Roth 指纹上实现了最高的描述性 ROC-AUC(接收者操作特征 - 曲线下面积)和 PR-AUC(曲线下精确召回面积),并且在分层分割下保持竞争力,ROC-AUC 为 0.9872。架构敏感性测试证实了跨多种网络配置的稳定 NDD 性能,ROC-AUC 值范围为 0.9876 至 0.9890。与无 Hessian 优化相比,在相同的仅 CPU 配置下,NDD 将 Klekota-Roth 上的每次运行时间从大约 50-54 秒减少到大约 8 秒,同时实现了相当的排名性能。这项工作的新颖性在于首次系统评估 H9N2 神经虚拟筛选的 NDD,证明正对角线曲率感知缩放在稀疏高维配体筛选设置中提供了实用、稳定且计算高效的优化替代方案,尽管在实际 抗病毒药物 优先级排序之前仍然需要外部验证和前瞻性实验确认。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 A/H9N2 造成持续的人畜共患病和兽医威胁,但用于 抗病毒药物 化合物优先级排序的有效计算工具仍然不发达,特别是在高维神经筛选中的​​优化器行为方面。
设计与对象
本研究提出牛顿差分(NDD)优化作为一种​​针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的新型优化器,目的是评估其在对齐的分子指纹表示和化学结构验证协议中的性能。
核心方法
由 615 种候选活性化合物和 844 种诱饵组成的 1,459 个分子的对齐基准由 EState(79 个特征)、PubChem(881 个特征)和 Klekota–Roth(4,860 个特征)指纹代表,共享相同的分子身份、标签和分割分配。固定多层感知器 (MLP) 分类器使用 NDD 和七个基线优化器在分层、支架键和相似性聚类分割协议下跨五个重复种子进行训练。
主要结果
NDD 在支架关键和相似性聚类协议下在 Klekota–Roth 指纹上实现了最高的描述性 ROC-AUC 和 PR-AUC,并且在分层分割下保持竞争力,ROC-AUC 为 0.9872。架构敏感性测试证实了跨多种网络配置的稳定 NDD 性能,ROC-AUC 值范围为 0.9876 至 0.9890。与无 Hessian 优化相比,在相同的仅 CPU 配置下,NDD 将 Klekota-Roth 上的每次运行时间从大约 50-54 秒减少到大约 8 秒,同时实现了相当的排名性能。
解释与创新
这项工作的新颖性在于首次对 H9N2 神经虚拟筛选的 NDD 进行系统评估,证明正对角线曲率感知缩放在基于稀疏高维配体的筛选设置中提供了实用、稳定且计算高效的优化替代方案。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的 抗病毒药物 化合物优先排序提供了一种计算有效的优化策略,这可以加速针对这种人畜共患威胁的潜在治疗方法的发现。
局限与证据强度
在实施实际的 抗病毒药物 优先级排序之前,外部验证和前瞻性实验确认仍然是必要的。由于研究的计算性质和实验验证的需要,证据强度中等。

Newton Divided Difference Optimization for Fingerprint-Based Neural Virtual Screening against Avian Influenza A/H9N2

Authors: Siti Amiroch
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza A/H9N2 poses persistent zoonotic and veterinary threats, yet efficient computational tools for antiviral compound prioritization remain underdeveloped, particularly with respect to optimizer behavior in high-dimensional neural screening. This study proposes Newton Divided Difference (NDD) optimization, a lightweight positive diagonal curvature-aware training strategy, as a novel optimizer for fingerprint-based neural virtual screening against avian influenza A/H9N2, with the objective of evaluating its performance across aligned molecular fingerprint representations and chemically structured validation protocols. An aligned benchmark of 1,459 molecules consisting of 615 candidate active compounds and 844 decoys was represented by EState (79 features), PubChem (881 features), and Klekota–Roth (4,860 features) fingerprints, sharing identical molecule identities, labels, and split assignments. A fixed multilayer perceptron (MLP) classifier was trained with NDD and seven baseline optimizers under stratified, scaffold-key, and similarity-cluster split protocols across five repeated seeds. NDD achieved the highest descriptive ROC-AUC (Receiver Operating Characteriztic – Area Under the Curve) and PR-AUC (Precision-Recall Area Under the Curve) on Klekota–Roth fingerprints under scaffold-key and similarity-cluster protocols, and remained competitive under the stratified split with ROC-AUC of 0.9872. Architecture-sensitivity tests confirmed stable NDD performance across multiple network configurations, with ROC-AUC values ranging from 0.9876 to 0.9890. Compared with Hessian-free optimization, NDD reduced per-run runtime from approximately 50–54 seconds to approximately 8 seconds on Klekota–Roth under the same CPU-only configuration while achieving comparable ranking performance. The novelty of this work lies in the first systematic assessment of NDD for H9N2 neural virtual screening, demonstrating that positive diagonal curvature-aware scaling provides a practical, stable, and computationally efficient optimization alternative in sparse high-dimensional ligand-based screening settings, although external validation and prospective experimental confirmation remain necessary before practical antiviral prioritization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza A/H9N2 poses persistent zoonotic and veterinary threats, yet efficient computational tools for antiviral compound prioritization remain underdeveloped, particularly with respect to optimizer behavior in high-dimensional neural screening.
Design and population
This study proposes Newton Divided Difference (NDD) optimization as a novel optimizer for fingerprint-based neural virtual screening against avian influenza A/H9N2, with the objective of evaluating its performance across aligned molecular fingerprint representations and chemically structured validation protocols.
Core methods
An aligned benchmark of 1,459 molecules consisting of 615 candidate active compounds and 844 decoys was represented by EState (79 features), PubChem (881 features), and Klekota–Roth (4,860 features) fingerprints, sharing identical molecule identities, labels, and split assignments. A fixed multilayer perceptron (MLP) classifier was trained with NDD and seven baseline optimizers under stratified, scaffold-key, and similarity-cluster split protocols across five repeated seeds.
Main results
NDD achieved the highest descriptive ROC-AUC and PR-AUC on Klekota–Roth fingerprints under scaffold-key and similarity-cluster protocols, and remained competitive under the stratified split with ROC-AUC of 0.9872. Architecture-sensitivity tests confirmed stable NDD performance across multiple network configurations, with ROC-AUC values ranging from 0.9876 to 0.9890. Compared with Hessian-free optimization, NDD reduced per-run runtime from approximately 50–54 seconds to approximately 8 seconds on Klekota–Roth under the same CPU-only configuration while achieving comparable ranking performance.
Interpretation and novelty
The novelty of this work lies in the first systematic assessment of NDD for H9N2 neural virtual screening, demonstrating that positive diagonal curvature-aware scaling provides a practical, stable, and computationally efficient optimization alternative in sparse high-dimensional ligand-based screening settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a computationally efficient optimization strategy for antiviral compound prioritization against avian influenza A/H9N2, which could accelerate the discovery of potential therapeutics for this zoonotic threat.
Limitations and evidence strength
External validation and prospective experimental confirmation remain necessary before practical antiviral prioritization can be implemented. The evidence strength is moderate due to the computational nature of the study and the need for experimental validation.

威斯康星州奶牛中 2.3.4.4b 基因型 D1.1 H5N1 病毒的分离和表征

Isolation and characterization of a clade 2.3.4.4b genotype D1.1 H5N1 virus from dairy cattle in Wisconsin
作者： Daria Mezhenskaia, Lavanya Babujee, Ailam Lim, Lizheng Guan, Dani Nguyen, Chunyang Gu, Gabriele Neumann, Keith Poulsen, Amie J. Eisfeld, Yoshihiro Kawaoka
摘要中文翻译摘要 在禽类 储存宿主 发生多次 跨物种溢出 事件后,最近在美国奶牛中检测到进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) (HPAI H5N1) 病毒。2025 年 12 月,通过国家牛奶检测策略,在威斯康星州的一个奶牛群中发现了 HPAI H5N1 病毒。在这里,我们报告从与受影响牛群相关的散装牛奶中分离出分支 2.3.4.4b、基因型 D1.1 H5N1 病毒 A/奶牛/威斯康星州/25G05743-001/2025 (WI5743-H5N1),描述其系统发育关系,并评估其对小鼠的致病性。在含胚鸡蛋中盲传后,回收了传染性病毒。系统发育分析表明,WI5743-H5N1 与之前报道的在内华达州和亚利桑那州奶牛中检测到的 D1.1 病毒不同,支持独立引入牛体内,并表明可能是当地鸟类来源。与密切相关的禽类病毒相比,WI5743-H5N1 编码了适应哺乳动物的取代 PB2-E627K 以及 HA、PB1-F2 和 NS1 中的额外氨基酸差异。在小鼠中,WI5743-H5N1 在呼吸组织中有效复制,并且在大脑中可检测到,但相对于其他最近的进化枝 2.3.4.4b、基因型 B3.13 病毒,其致死率较低。总之,这些发现突出了感染奶牛的 HPAI H5N1 病毒的遗传和表型多样性,并强调了持续 监测 和新兴病毒株功能表征的重要性。重要性 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 病毒最近通过禽类 跨物种溢出 事件进入美国奶牛,为哺乳动物的病毒适应创造了新的机会。在这里,我们描述了从 2025 年 12 月威斯康星州 跨物种溢出 事件期间收集的散装牛奶中分离和表征的进化枝 2.3.4.4b(基因型 D1.1 H5N1 病毒)。系统发育分析表明,该病毒代表独立引入奶牛,与之前报道的内华达州和亚利桑那州发现的 D1.1 病毒不同。尽管该病毒编码了适应哺乳动物的PB2-E627K取代,但它在小鼠中表现出相对较低的致死率,凸显了H5N1病毒的哺乳动物适应和致病性的复杂性。这些发现扩展了目前对感染奶牛的 H5N1 病毒的遗传和表型多样性的理解,并强调了持续 监测 和新兴毒株功能表征的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒最近从禽类 储存宿主 传播到美国奶牛中,引发了人们对哺乳动物适应和公共卫生风险的担忧。该研究旨在表征从威斯康星州奶牛群中新分离出的 H5N1 病毒,以了解其遗传和表型特性。
设计与对象
该研究从 2025 年 12 月从威斯康星州奶牛群收集的散装牛奶中分离并鉴定了分支 2.3.4.4b、基因型 D1.1 H5N1 病毒(A/奶牛/威斯康星州/25G05743-001/2025)。
核心方法
通过在含胚鸡蛋中盲传代来回收传染性病毒。确定了系统发育关系,并评估了该病毒的哺乳动物适应性取代和对小鼠的致病性。
主要结果
系统发育分析显示,WI5743-H5N1 与之前报道的内华达州和亚利桑那州奶牛中的 D1.1 病毒不同,表明可能是从当地鸟类来源独立引入的。该病毒编码了适应哺乳动物的 PB2-E627K 取代以及 HA、PB1-F2 和 NS1 中的其他氨基酸差异。在小鼠中,WI5743-H5N1 在呼吸组织中有效复制,在大脑中可检测到,并且表现出比最近的进化枝 2.3.4.4b(基因型 B3.13 病毒)更低的致死率。
解释与创新
研究结果证明了感染奶牛的 H5N1 病毒的遗传和表型多样性,并有独立 跨物种溢出 事件和哺乳动物适应 突变 的证据。该研究强调了预测新出现的 H5N1 病毒株的哺乳动物致病性的复杂性。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调需要继续对奶牛中的 H5N1 病毒进行 监测 和功能表征,以便为潜在人畜共患 传播 的风险评估和防范提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于它只关注单一菌株和小鼠模型,可能无法完全代表人类或牛的感染动态。由于样本量小且缺乏人体临床数据,证据强度中等。

Isolation and characterization of a clade 2.3.4.4b genotype D1.1 H5N1 virus from dairy cattle in Wisconsin

Authors: Daria Mezhenskaia, Lavanya Babujee, Ailam Lim, Lizheng Guan, Dani Nguyen, Chunyang Gu, Gabriele Neumann, Keith Poulsen, Amie J. Eisfeld, Yoshihiro Kawaoka
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) (HPAI H5N1) viruses of clade 2.3.4.4b have recently been detected in U.S. dairy cattle following multiple spillover events from avian reservoirs. In December 2025, HPAI H5N1 virus was identified in a dairy herd in Wisconsin through the National Milk Testing Strategy. Here, we report the isolation of a clade 2.3.4.4b, genotype D1.1 H5N1 virus, A/dairy cow/Wisconsin/25G05743-001/2025 (WI5743-H5N1), from bulk milk associated with the affected herd, describe its phylogenetic relationships, and assess its pathogenicity in mice. Infectious virus was recovered following blind passage in embryonated chicken eggs. Phylogenetic analysis demonstrated that WI5743-H5N1 is distinct from previously reported D1.1 viruses detected in dairy cattle in Nevada and Arizona, supporting an independent introduction into cattle, and indicating a likely local avian source. Compared with closely related avian viruses, WI5743-H5N1 encoded the mammalian-adapting substitution PB2-E627K and additional amino acid differences in HA, PB1-F2, and NS1. In mice, WI5743-H5N1 replicated efficiently in respiratory tissues and was detectable in the brain but exhibited lower lethality relative to other recent clade 2.3.4.4b, genotype B3.13 viruses. Together, these findings highlight the genetic and phenotypic diversity of HPAI H5N1 viruses infecting dairy cattle and underscore the importance of continued surveillance and functional characterization of emerging strains. IMPORTANCE Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) viruses have recently entered U.S. dairy cattle through multiple spillover events from avian reservoirs, creating new opportunities for viral adaptation in mammals. Here, we describe the isolation and characterization of a clade 2.3.4.4b, genotype D1.1 H5N1 virus from bulk milk collected during a spillover event in Wisconsin in December 2025. Phylogenetic analyses demonstrated that this virus represents an independent introduction into dairy cattle distinct from previously reported D1.1 viruses identified in Nevada and Arizona. Although the virus encoded the mammalian-adapting PB2-E627K substitution, it exhibited comparatively low lethality in mice, highlighting the complexity of mammalian adaptation and pathogenicity in H5N1 viruses. These findings expand current understanding of the genetic and phenotypic diversity of H5N1 viruses infecting dairy cattle and emphasize the importance of continued surveillance and functional characterization of emerging strains.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have recently spilled over into U.S. dairy cattle from avian reservoirs, raising concerns about mammalian adaptation and public health risk. The study aimed to characterize a newly isolated H5N1 virus from a Wisconsin dairy herd to understand its genetic and phenotypic properties.
Design and population
The study isolated and characterized a clade 2.3.4.4b, genotype D1.1 H5N1 virus (A/dairy cow/Wisconsin/25G05743-001/2025) from bulk milk collected in December 2025 from a Wisconsin dairy herd identified through the National Milk Testing Strategy.
Core methods
Infectious virus was recovered by blind passage in embryonated chicken eggs. Phylogenetic relationships were determined, and the virus was assessed for mammalian-adapting substitutions and pathogenicity in mice.
Main results
Phylogenetic analysis showed WI5743-H5N1 is distinct from previously reported D1.1 viruses in dairy cattle from Nevada and Arizona, indicating an independent introduction likely from a local avian source. The virus encoded the mammalian-adapting PB2-E627K substitution and additional amino acid differences in HA, PB1-F2, and NS1. In mice, WI5743-H5N1 replicated efficiently in respiratory tissues, was detectable in the brain, and exhibited lower lethality than recent clade 2.3.4.4b, genotype B3.13 viruses.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate genetic and phenotypic diversity among H5N1 viruses infecting dairy cattle, with evidence of independent spillover events and mammalian-adapting mutations. The study highlights the complexity of predicting mammalian pathogenicity in emerging H5N1 strains.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results underscore the need for continued surveillance and functional characterization of H5N1 viruses in dairy cattle to inform risk assessment and preparedness for potential zoonotic transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single isolate and mouse model, which may not fully represent human or bovine infection dynamics. The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and absence of human clinical data.

聚乙二醇化聚天冬酰胺纳米胶束递送自扩增 禽流感 mRNA 疫苗

PEGylated PolyaspartamideNanomicelle Delivery ofSelf-amplifying Avian Influenza mRNA Vaccine
作者： Chin-Yu Lin, Cheng-Chung Chang, Long Yi Chan, Shih‐Hsuan Chan, Yung‐Luen Yu, Yueh-Chiang Lin, Minoru Tsuzuki, Ya-Mei Chen, Guan‐Yu Zhuo, Hsi‐Kai Tsou
摘要中文翻译摘要 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 对全球家禽业和公共卫生构成重大威胁,凸显了有效 疫苗 的需求。目前,人用流感疫苗是由鸡蛋生产的灭活疫苗,其生产过程耗时且安全。信使RNA (mRNA)是细胞的转录产物,可以直接产生蛋白质,不存在关于DNA模板残基或基因组DNA中整合的突变的安全问题。最近,使用 mRNA 表达 疫苗 抗原开发的 COVID-19 疫苗 已产生有效的体液和细胞 免疫应答。此外,mRNA 可以诱导强大的细胞 Toll 样受体 免疫应答,并有潜力作为一种有前途的 疫苗 佐剂。在这项研究中,我们开发了封装表达 AIV 抗原的 mRNA 的聚乙二醇化聚氨基复合物纳米胶束。这些纳米胶束被制造为 AIV 疫苗,并在体内评估了它们的功效。我们生产了一种高度翻译效率、自我扩增的 mRNA (saRNA),它表达 AIV 的主要抗原、血凝素 (HA) 蛋白。随后,我们使用聚乙二醇化聚天冬酰胺嵌段共聚物将表达 HA 的 saRNA 封装在自组装聚合物纳米胶束中。然后,我们比较了小鼠体内装载 mRNA 的纳米胶束给药方案以及 疫苗 引发的体液(抗体滴度和血凝抑制能力)和细胞 免疫应答(干扰素-γ [IFN-γ] 和白细胞介素 4 [IL-4] 细胞因子)。我们的研究结果表明,利用聚氨基自组装纳米胶束成功开发了禽类mRNA 疫苗,显着增强了免疫应答针对AIV抗原的体液和细胞能力,为未来进一步改进禽流感 mRNA 疫苗提供了有价值的参考。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于AIV对家禽和公共卫生构成全球威胁,该研究解决了对改良禽流感病毒(AIV)疫苗的迫切需求。它强调了当前基于灭活鸡蛋的 疫苗 的局限性,并探索 mRNA 疫苗 技术作为一种有前途的替代方案。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种自我扩增的 mRNA 疫苗,表达封装在聚乙二醇化聚天冬酰胺纳米胶束中的 AIV 血凝素 (HA) 蛋白。通过比较小鼠体内的体液和细胞 免疫应答 来评估 疫苗 的体内功效。
核心方法
使用聚乙二醇化聚天冬酰胺嵌段共聚物,生产了表达 AIV HA 蛋白的高度翻译效率、自扩增 mRNA (saRNA),并将其封装在自组装聚合物纳米胶束中。该研究比较了 疫苗 在小鼠中引发的体液(抗体滴度和血凝抑制能力)和细胞 免疫应答(IFN-γ 和 IL-4 细胞因子)。
主要结果
结果表明,使用聚氨基自组装纳米胶束的禽类 mRNA 疫苗 显着增强了小鼠体内针对 AIV 抗原的体液和细胞 免疫应答。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为纳米胶束封装的 saRNA 疫苗 平台有效诱导强大的 免疫应答 的证据,这表明了一种改善 禽流感 mRNA 疫苗 的有价值的方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持基于 mRNA 的纳米胶束 疫苗 作为 禽流感 预防平台的潜力,为传统的基于鸡蛋的灭活 疫苗 提供更快且可能更安全的替代品。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于小鼠体内研究,缺乏人类临床数据,这限制了对家禽或人类的普遍适用性。由于缺乏更广泛的验证,证据的强度中等。

PEGylated PolyaspartamideNanomicelle Delivery ofSelf-amplifying Avian Influenza mRNA Vaccine

Authors: Chin-Yu Lin, Cheng-Chung Chang, Long Yi Chan, Shih‐Hsuan Chan, Yung‐Luen Yu, Yueh-Chiang Lin, Minoru Tsuzuki, Ya-Mei Chen, Guan‐Yu Zhuo, Hsi‐Kai Tsou
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The avian influenza virus (AIV) poses a major threat to the poultry industry and public health worldwide, highlighting the need for effective vaccines. Currently, the influenza vaccine for human use is an inactivated vaccine produced from eggs, which requires time-consuming and safe production processes. Messenger RNA (mRNA) is a transcriptional product of cells that can directly produce proteins, with no safety concerns regarding DNA template residues or integrated mutations in the genomic DNA. Recently, COVID-19 vaccines developed using mRNA to express the vaccine antigen have resulted in effective humoral and cellular immune responses. Additionally, mRNA can induce a robust cellular Toll-like receptor immune response and has the potential to serve as a promising vaccine adjuvant. In this study, we developed PEGylated polyamino polyplex nanomicelles encapsulating mRNA that expresses the AIV antigen. These nanomicelles were manufactured as an AIV vaccine, and their efficacy was evaluated in vivo. We produced a highly translation-effective, self-amplifying mRNA (saRNA) that expresses the main antigen, hemagglutinin (HA) protein of AIV. Subsequently, we encapsulated the HA-expressing saRNA in self-assembled polymeric nanomicelles using a PEGylated polyaspartamide block copolymer. Then, we compared the mRNA-loaded nanomicelle administration scenarios and the vaccine-elicited humoral (antibody titer and hemagglutination inhibition capability) and cellular immune responses (interferon-gamma [IFN-γ] and interleukin-4 [IL-4] cytokines) in mice. Our results demonstrated the successful development of an avian mRNA vaccine using polyamino self-assembled nanomicelles, which significantly enhanced both the humoral and cellular immune responses against the AIV antigen, providing a valuable reference for the further improvement of avian influenza mRNA vaccines in the future.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the urgent need for improved avian influenza virus (AIV) vaccines due to the global threat posed by AIV to poultry and public health. It highlights limitations of current inactivated egg-based vaccines and explores mRNA vaccine technology as a promising alternative.
Design and population
The research developed a self-amplifying mRNA vaccine expressing the hemagglutinin (HA) protein of AIV encapsulated in PEGylated polyaspartamide nanomicelles. The vaccine's efficacy was evaluated in vivo by comparing humoral and cellular immune responses in mice.
Core methods
A highly translation-effective, self-amplifying mRNA (saRNA) expressing the HA protein of AIV was produced and encapsulated in self-assembled polymeric nanomicelles using a PEGylated polyaspartamide block copolymer. The study compared vaccine-elicited humoral (antibody titer and hemagglutination inhibition capability) and cellular immune responses (IFN-γ and IL-4 cytokines) in mice.
Main results
The results demonstrated that the avian mRNA vaccine using polyamino self-assembled nanomicelles significantly enhanced both humoral and cellular immune responses against the AIV antigen in mice.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that the nanomicelle-encapsulated saRNA vaccine platform effectively induces robust immune responses, suggesting a valuable approach for improving avian influenza mRNA vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the potential of mRNA-based nanomicelle vaccines as a platform for avian influenza prevention, offering a faster and potentially safer alternative to traditional egg-based inactivated vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to in vivo mouse studies and lacks human clinical data, which constrains generalizability to poultry or human populations. The strength of evidence is moderate due to the absence of broader validation.

新西兰花了数年时间准备应对 H5N1 禽流感。对于某些物种来说,这可能还不够

NZ has spent years preparing for H5N1 bird flu. For some species, it may not be enough
作者： Tammy Steeves
摘要中文翻译随着试验的完成,新西兰已出现H5N1禽流感疫情,疫苗接种随即启动,首批涉及研究中五个物种的300只个体。上月,高致病性H5N1禽流感毒株抵达奥特亚罗瓦(Aotearoa)新西兰,引发了一项酝酿多年的全国性应对措施。到那时,由初级产业部牵头的协调应对已建立监测系统、物种保护计划,甚至为某些鸟类制定了疫苗。然而即便如此,这些准备可能仍不足以挽救该国一些最稀有鸟类。许多物种仅存活于微小且隔离的种群中,遗传多样性有限。对于这些高度脆弱的物种,结局可能不仅取决于保护工作,还取决于运气。遗传多样性是自然对疾病的“保险政策”。在大种群中,部分个体更可能在暴发中存活并帮助种群恢复。但在遗传多样性极低的小型隔离种群中,这些概率大幅降低,使物种面临更高的灭绝风险。为何疫苗只能发挥有限作用 H5N1在2020年出现以来,已在全球多地引发多起大规模死亡率事件,成为多种物种数十年来面临的最严重疾病威胁之一。疫苗提供了一层重要的保护,但并非万能良方。由保护部门牵头的疫苗试验涉及五种密集管理的濒危物种,发现有效保护至少需要间隔数周接种两剂疫苗。在黑鹳(kakī)——一种极度濒危的黑鹮及全球最稀有涉禽之一——免疫力在三个月后下降,意味着需要接种第三剂。然而,更大的挑战在于如何将疫苗...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了为什么新西兰现有的 H5N1 防备措施可能不足以应对其最脆弱的鸟类物种,尽管进行了多年的国家规划和 监测 系统。
设计与对象
该分析综合了由第一产业部和保护部领导的国家 监测 系统、保护计划和 疫苗 试验,重点关注遗传多样性有限的受威胁鸟类物种。
核心方法
该论文回顾了针对五种集中管理的受威胁物种的 疫苗 试验方案,包括给药时间表和免疫持续时间,并评估了在野生种群中提供多剂量 疫苗接种 的实际限制。
主要结果
疫苗 的有效性需要至少两剂,服用 kakī 三个月后免疫力下降,需要第三剂。实际挑战包括重新捕获鸟类以进行后续剂量的困难以及平衡跨物种和地点的有限资源的困难。
解释与创新
作者强调,虽然 疫苗 提供了关键的保护,但它们在现实世界中的应用受到后勤挑战和物种特定风险的限制,强调需要有针对性的保护策略,而不是一揽子 疫苗接种。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,疫苗接种 工作应优先考虑具有可行重新捕获率的物种,并重点关注高风险时期,例如殖民地筑巢季节,以减轻 H5N1 对新西兰最稀有鸟类的影响。
局限与证据强度
证据是观察性的,基于对五个物种的单一 疫苗 试验,缺乏更广泛的群体水平数据或随机比较,这限制了推论的普遍性和强度。

NZ has spent years preparing for H5N1 bird flu. For some species, it may not be enough

Authors: Tammy Steeves
Original abstract (English)
With the trial now complete and H5N1 in New Zealand, vaccinations are underway, beginning with 300 individuals from the five species involved in the study. The arrival of the highly pathogenic H5N1 strain of bird flu in Aotearoa New Zealand last month triggered a national response years in the making. By then, surveillance systems, species protection plans and even vaccines for some birds were already in place as part of a coordinated approach led by the Ministry for Primary Industries. Yet even these preparations may not be enough to save some of the country’s rarest birds. Many survive in tiny, isolated populations with limited genetic diversity. For these highly vulnerable species, the outcome may depend not only on conservation efforts, but also on chance. Genetic diversity acts as nature’s insurance policy against disease. In large populations, some individuals are more likely to survive an outbreak and help the population recover. But in small, isolated populations with little genetic diversity, those odds are much lower, leaving species far more vulnerable to extinction. Why vaccines can only do so much H5N1 has caused mass mortality events in wild birds around the world since emerging in 2020, making it one of the most significant disease threats many species have faced in decades. Vaccines offer one important layer of protection, but they are no silver bullet. A Department of Conservation-led vaccine trial involving five intensively managed threatened species found that effective protection requires at least two doses administered a few weeks apart. In kakī – the critically endangered black stilt and one of the world’s rarest wading birds – immunity declined after three months, meaning a third dose will be needed. The greater challenge, however, is delivering...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study examines why New Zealand’s existing H5N1 preparedness may be insufficient for its most vulnerable bird species despite years of national planning and surveillance systems.
Design and population
The analysis synthesizes national surveillance systems, conservation plans, and vaccine trials led by the Ministry for Primary Industries and Department of Conservation, focusing on threatened bird species with limited genetic diversity.
Core methods
The paper reviews vaccine trial protocols for five intensively managed threatened species, including dosing schedules and immunity duration, and evaluates practical constraints in delivering multi-dose vaccination in wild populations.
Main results
Vaccine effectiveness requires at least two doses, with immunity declining after three months in kakī, necessitating a third dose. Practical challenges include the difficulty of recapturing birds for subsequent doses and balancing limited resources across species and locations.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors highlight that while vaccines provide critical protection, their real-world application is constrained by logistical challenges and species-specific risks, underscoring the need for targeted conservation strategies rather than blanket vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize prioritizing vaccination efforts for species with feasible recapture rates and focusing on high-risk periods, such as colonial nesting seasons, to mitigate H5N1 impacts on New Zealand’s rarest birds.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is observational and based on a single vaccine trial with five species, lacking broader population-level data or randomized comparisons, which limits generalizability and strength of inference.

危地马拉圣马科斯里奥布兰科地区的 H5N2 禽流感血清警戒

Serovigilancia de influenza aviar H5N2 en patos y gansos domésticos de Río Blanco, San Marcos, Guatemala
作者： Carlos Andreé Bran-Gaytán, Carlos Camey, Francisco Escobar-Serrano
摘要中文翻译对危地马拉西部高地圣马科斯里奥布兰科市的家鸭和家鹅进行了血清学监测。采集83只番鸭(Cairina moschata)和29只家鹅(Anser anser var. Domesticus)共112份血清样品,通过血凝抑制试验,测定H5N2低致病性血清流行率(LPAI)的血清流行率。确定 血清流行率 点为 0.89%,112 只番鸭中只有 1 只显示出对该疾病呈阳性的抗体。结果表明,未接受动物卫生服务机构疫苗接种的鸭子体内存在针对 H5N2 LPAI 的抗体,这表明该鸭子曾接触过该病毒,并且该地区可能存在病毒传播。因此,有必要消除障碍,在该国实施适当的 监测 和控制策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估危地马拉圣马科斯里奥布兰科家鸭和鹅中 H5N2 低致病性 血清流行率 (LPAI) 的 血清流行率,以了解其潜在传播和对鸟类种群的影响。
设计与对象
在危地马拉圣马科斯的里奥布兰科进行了血清学调查,从83只番鸭(Cairina moschata)和29只家鹅(Anser anser var.)采集了112份血清样本。
核心方法
H5N2 LPAI 的 血清流行率 是通过对从特定家禽种群采集的血清样品进行血凝抑制试验来测定的。
主要结果
H5N2 LPAI 的 血清流行率 点为 0.89%,112 只番鸭中只有 1 只表现出病毒抗体阳性。
解释与创新
未接种疫苗的鸭子体内出现抗体表明可能接触过该病毒,并表明该地区可能存在 H5N2 LPAI 传播,这是该地区的一项新发现。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调需要加强 监测 和控制策略来管理 H5N2 LPAI 在家禽种群中的潜在传播,这可能对公共卫生和动物福利产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于它只关注单个城市并使用单一血清学检测,可能无法完全掌握 H5N2 LPAI 的传播范围。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Serovigilancia de influenza aviar H5N2 en patos y gansos domésticos de Río Blanco, San Marcos, Guatemala

Authors: Carlos Andreé Bran-Gaytán, Carlos Camey, Francisco Escobar-Serrano
Original abstract (English)
Serological monitoring was conducted in domestic ducks and geese from the municipality of Río Blanco, San Marcos, in Guatemala’s western highlands. A total of 112 serum samples were taken from 83 Muscovy ducks (Cairina moschata) and 29 domestic geese (Anser anser var. domesticus) to determine the seroprevalence of H5N2 Low Pathogenic Avian Influenza (LPAI), by the haemagglutination inhibition test. A point seroprevalence of 0.89% was determined, with only 1 Muscovy duck out of 112 showing antibodies considered positive for the disease. The results indicate the presence of antibodies against H5N2 LPAI in a duck not vaccinated by the animal health service, suggesting exposure to the virus and possible circulation in the area. Therefore, it is necessary to remove obstacles to implement adequate surveillance and control strategies in the country.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the seroprevalence of H5N2 Low Pathogenic Avian Influenza (LPAI) in domestic ducks and geese in Río Blanco, San Marcos, Guatemala, to understand its potential circulation and impact on avian populations.
Design and population
A serological survey was conducted in Río Blanco, San Marcos, Guatemala, from which 112 serum samples were collected from 83 Muscovy ducks (Cairina moschata) and 29 domestic geese (Anser anser var.
Core methods
Seroprevalence of H5N2 LPAI was determined using the haemagglutination inhibition test on serum samples collected from the specified domestic bird populations.
Main results
The point seroprevalence of H5N2 LPAI was found to be 0.89%, with only one Muscovy duck out of 112 showing positive antibodies for the virus.
Interpretation and novelty
The presence of antibodies in a non-vaccinated duck suggests possible exposure to the virus and indicates the potential circulation of H5N2 LPAI in the area, which is a novel finding for this region.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need for enhanced surveillance and control strategies to manage the potential spread of H5N2 LPAI in domestic bird populations, which could have implications for public health and animal welfare.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single municipality and the use of a single serological test, which may not fully capture the extent of H5N2 LPAI circulation. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these constraints.

保守的甲型流感表位候选区域和 ESM-2 序列特征的基准

Conserved influenza A epitope candidate regions and a benchmark of ESM-2 sequence features
作者： Qingxiu Li, Zhenjun Li
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒抗原漂移迫使每年重新配制 疫苗,从而激发了对可支持广泛保护性 疫苗 的保守候选表位的搜索。我们系统地筛选了甲型流感病毒序列(H1N1、H3N2、H5N1;九种病毒蛋白),以确定 98 个保守候选区域,其中 38 个在 H1N1、H3N2 和 H5N1 共有序列中是相同的,全部位于聚合酶复合物和核蛋白(PB2、PB1、PA、NP)中,而 10 个表面糖蛋白(HA/NA)候选区域是亚型特异性的。然后,我们对两个蛋白质语言模型 (ESM-2) 特征与对齐保守性进行了基准测试。组屏蔽对数概率与 MSA 保守性适度相关(对于 HA,Spearman rho = 0.25 至 0.39),但没有为 T 细胞表位辨别提供增量值(AUROC 变化 +0.004,p = 0.46);注意力衍生的接触密度并不是有效的溶剂可及性代理。精心设计的抗体表位基准(22 个簇,5 个支持中和)对于高置信度 B 细胞测试来说动力不足。我们记录数据质量和可重复性缺陷(长度异质性、坐标映射和伪复制)并发布可审核的基准。这些结果提供了可审核的候选资源,并表明,在评估的基准中,ESM-2 序列评分并没有改善除比对衍生的保护之外的表位优先顺序。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在鉴定甲型流感病毒中的保守候选表位,以支持广泛保护性 疫苗 的开发,因为抗原漂移需要每年重新配制 疫苗。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对多种亚型和蛋白质的甲型流感病毒序列进行系统筛选,重点关注聚合酶复合物和核蛋白内的保守区域。
核心方法
该研究使用群体屏蔽对数概率和注意力衍生接触密度作为指标,对 ESM-2 序列特征与比对保守进行基准测试。
主要结果
组屏蔽对数概率与 MSA 保守性显示出中等相关性,但没有改善 T 细胞表位辨别力,而注意力衍生的接触密度并不是有效的溶剂可及性代理。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在评估的基准中,ESM-2 序列评分并未增强表位优先级,超越传统的比对衍生保护方法。
科研与公卫意义
结果为候选表位提供了可审核的资源,并强调了使用 ESM-2 特征对甲型流感病毒进行表位优先排序的局限性。
局限与证据强度
该研究指出了数据质量和重现性问题,例如长度异质性和假复制,并承认抗体表位基准对于高置信度 B 细胞测试来说动力不足。

Conserved influenza A epitope candidate regions and a benchmark of ESM-2 sequence features

Authors: Qingxiu Li, Zhenjun Li
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus antigenic drift forces annual vaccine reformulation, motivating the search for conserved epitope candidates that could support broadly protective vaccines. We systematically screened influenza A virus sequences (H1N1, H3N2, H5N1; nine viral proteins) to define 98 conserved candidate regions, 38 of which were identical across the H1N1, H3N2, and H5N1 consensus sequences, all in the polymerase complex and nucleoprotein (PB2, PB1, PA, NP), whereas the ten surface-glycoprotein (HA/NA) candidates were subtype-specific. We then benchmarked two protein-language-model (ESM-2) features against alignment conservation. Group-masked log-probability correlated moderately with MSA conservation (Spearman rho = 0.25 to 0.39 for HA) but provided no incremental value for T-cell epitope discrimination (change in AUROC +0.004, p = 0.46); attention-derived contact-density was not a valid solvent-accessibility proxy. A curated antibody-epitope benchmark (22 clusters, 5 neutralization-supported) was underpowered for a high-confidence B-cell test. We document data-quality and reproducibility pitfalls (length heterogeneity, coordinate mapping, and pseudoreplication) and release the auditable benchmark. These results provide an auditable candidate resource and show that, in the evaluated benchmarks, ESM-2 sequence scores did not improve epitope prioritization beyond alignment-derived conservation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify conserved epitope candidates in influenza A viruses to support the development of broadly protective vaccines, as antigenic drift necessitates annual vaccine reformulation.
Design and population
The research involved a systematic screening of influenza A virus sequences across multiple subtypes and proteins, focusing on conserved regions within the polymerase complex and nucleoprotein.
Core methods
The study benchmarked ESM-2 sequence features against alignment conservation, using group-masked log-probability and attention-derived contact-density as metrics.
Main results
Group-masked log-probability showed moderate correlation with MSA conservation but did not improve T-cell epitope discrimination, while attention-derived contact-density was not a valid solvent-accessibility proxy.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that ESM-2 sequence scores do not enhance epitope prioritization beyond traditional alignment-derived conservation methods in the evaluated benchmarks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide an auditable resource for epitope candidates and highlight the limitations of using ESM-2 features for epitope prioritization in influenza A viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study notes data-quality and reproducibility issues, such as length heterogeneity and pseudoreplication, and acknowledges that the antibody-epitope benchmark was underpowered for high-confidence B-cell testing.

动物疾病如何流行:法国 禽流感 和意大利非洲猪瘟的案例研究。

How animal diseases become endemic: case studies of avian influenza in France and African swine fever in Italy.
作者： Frederick Keck
摘要中文翻译本文比较了法国对 高致病性禽流感 和意大利非洲猪瘟的处理,询问专家如何将这些动物流行病定性为地方病。虽然农民认为野生动物威胁着他们的家畜,但猎人和野生动物管理者将野生动物的行为视为监管的潜力。野生动物和家养动物之间的这种区别被经常从事狩猎活动的小规模农民所忽视,但支持工业化农业的工会以及省、国家和欧盟各级的卫生当局却强化了这种区别。另一方面,动物活动家则对这种区别感到困扰,因为他们将动物视为有感情的生物,通过同情心赋予它们权利或灵魂。因此,随着病毒揭示了人类与动物之间关系的不同方面,动物流行病产生了超越野生/驯化二分法的广泛观点。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述探讨了 禽流感 A 病毒(例如 H5N8、H5Nx)及其人畜共患 跨物种溢出 在法国如何被定性为地方病,重点关注专家如何将动物流行病定性为地方病以及人与动物关系在这一分类中的作用这一核心问题。
证据基础与方法
本文综合了关于野生/家养动物边界及其监管影响的定性观点,借鉴了专家和利益相关者的观点,而不是系统的数据库搜索或荟萃分析方法。
共识与结论
野生动物和家养动物之间的区别是社会建构的,并受到工业化农业工会和卫生当局的强化,同时受到强调动物感知的动物活动人士的挑战。
争议与缺口
文章强调了将动物流行病定性为地方病方面尚未解决的紧张局势,特别是在如何将野生动物行为和动物权利观点纳入监管框架方面缺乏共识。
科研与实践启示
审查表明,未来的动物流行病管理应考虑到不同利益相关者的观点,包括动物活动家的观点,以便更好地将监管实践与人与动物关系的现实结合起来。

How animal diseases become endemic: case studies of avian influenza in France and African swine fever in Italy.

Authors: Frederick Keck
Original abstract (English)
This article compares the management of highly pathogenic avian influenza in France and African swine fever in Italy, asking how experts have qualified these epizootics as endemics. While farmers perceive wild animals as threatening their domestic animals, hunters and wildlife managers understand the behaviour of wild animals as a potentiality for regulation. This distinction between wild and domestic is ignored by small-scale farmers who are often hunters, but it is reinforced by trade unions supporting industrial farming and by health authorities at provincial, national and European Union levels. Animal activists, on the other side, trouble this distinction because they take the perspective of animals as sentient beings, endowing them with rights or souls by their feeling of compassion. Epizootics thus produce a wide range of perspectives across and beyond wild/domesticated dichotomies as viruses reveal different aspects of relations between humans and animals.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines how avian influenza A viruses (e.g., H5N8, H5Nx) and their zoonotic spillovers are framed as endemic in France, focusing on the central question of how experts qualify epizootics as endemics and the role of human-animal relations in this classification.
Evidence base and method
The article synthesizes qualitative perspectives on wild/domestic animal boundaries and their regulatory implications, drawing on expert and stakeholder viewpoints rather than systematic database searches or meta-analytic methods.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the distinction between wild and domestic animals is socially constructed and reinforced by industrial farming trade unions and health authorities, while being challenged by animal activists who emphasize animal sentience.
Controversies and gaps
The article highlights unresolved tensions in framing epizootics as endemics, particularly the lack of consensus on how to integrate wild animal behavior and animal rights perspectives into regulatory frameworks.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future epizootic management should account for diverse stakeholder perspectives, including those of animal activists, to better align regulatory practices with the realities of human-animal relations.
